004376: def apply_monitor_policy_fields(app_item):
004377:     policy = get_monitor_policy(app_item.get("stack_key"))
004378:     group = select_primary_version_group(app_item)
004379:     available_tags = (
004380:         list((group.get("available") or {}).get("display_tags") or [])
004381:         if group else []
004382:     )
004383:     common_newer_tags = (
004384:         list((group.get("newer") or {}).get("display_tags") or [])
004385:         if group else []
004386:     )
004387: 
004388:     mode = policy.get("mode") or "fixed"
004389:     target_tag = str(policy.get("target_tag") or "").strip() or None
004390:     can_version_update = False
004391: 
004392:     current_aliases = {
004393:         _version_alias(value)
004394:         for value in ((group or {}).get("current_tags") or [])
004395:         if _version_alias(value)
004396:     }
004397: 
004398:     follow_tag_switch_only = False
004399:     follow_target_state = None
004400: 
004401:     if app_item.get("compose_project") and group:
004402:         if mode == "follow":
004403:             # A different tag name does not automatically mean a different image.
004404:             # If all mismatched members already have the exact :latest digest,
004405:             # suppress "Update installieren" and normalize only the Compose tag.
004406:             group_items = list((group or {}).get("items") or [])
004407:             mismatched = [
004408:                 item
004409:                 for item in group_items
004410:                 if str(_version_item_current_tag(item) or "").strip().lower()
004411:                 != FOLLOW_POLICY_TAG
004412:             ]
004413: 
004414:             if mismatched:
004415:                 target_states = [
004416:                     str(item.get("follow_target_state") or "unknown")
004417:                     for item in mismatched
004418:                 ]
004419:                 states = [item.get("follow_target_same_image") for item in mismatched]
004420:                 if any(value == "missing" for value in target_states):
004421:                     # At least one image in this version family has no :latest
004422:                     # tag.  A family-wide follow transition is therefore not
004423:                     # installable and must never be auto-attempted.
004424:                     follow_target_state = "unavailable"
004425:                     can_version_update = False
004426:                 elif states and all(value is True for value in states):
004427:                     follow_tag_switch_only = True
004428:                     follow_target_state = "same-image"
004429:                     can_version_update = False
004430:                 else:
004431:                     follow_target_state = (
004432:                         "different-image"
004433:                         if any(value is False for value in states)
004434:                         else "unknown"
004435:                     )
004436:                     can_version_update = True
004437:             else:
004438:                 follow_target_state = "already-following"
004439:                 can_version_update = False
004440: 
004441:         elif mode == "upgrade" and target_tag in available_tags:
004442:             # Manual selection may intentionally move backwards, but changing
004443:             # to the version already configured for the whole family is a no-op.
004444:             can_version_update = _version_alias(target_tag) not in current_aliases
004445: 
004446:     app_item["monitor_policy"] = policy
004447:     app_item["policy_available_tags"] = available_tags
004448:     app_item["policy_follow_tag"] = FOLLOW_POLICY_TAG
004449:     app_item["follow_tag_switch_only"] = follow_tag_switch_only
004450:     app_item["follow_target_state"] = follow_target_state
004451:     app_item["can_version_update"] = can_version_update
004452:     app_item["installed_version"] = _version_group_installed_tag(group) if group else None
004453:     app_item["version_group"] = _public_version_group(group)
004454:     app_item["version_group_state"] = (
004455:         "selected"
004456:         if group
004457:         else ("ambiguous" if len(build_version_groups(app_item)) > 1 else "none")
004458:     )
004459:     return app_item

014942: 
014943: 
014944: def _verify_restore_runtime(meta, container_names):
014945:     snapshots = _backup_container_runtime_map(meta)
014946:     verified = 0
014947:     for name in container_names:
014948:         expected = snapshots.get(name) or {}
014949:         expected_state = str(expected.get("state") or "").strip().lower()
014950:         if not expected_state:
014951:             expected_state = (
014952:                 "running"
014953:                 if name in set(meta.get("running_containers_before_backup") or [])
014954:                 else "exited"
014955:             )
014956:         try:
014957:             baseline_restarts = int(expected.get("restart_count") or 0)
014958:         except Exception:
014959:             baseline_restarts = 0
014960:         try:
014961:             expected_exit = expected.get("exit_code")
014962:             expected_exit = None if expected_exit is None else int(expected_exit)
014963:         except Exception:
014964:             expected_exit = None
014965: 
014966:         try:
014967:             wait_for_image_source_runtime_stable(
014968:                 name,
014969:                 timeout=120,
014970:                 stable_seconds=12,
014971:                 baseline_restart_count=baseline_restarts,
014972:                 expected_state=expected_state,
014973:                 expected_exit_code=expected_exit,
014974:                 expected_runtime_snapshot={
014975:                     "health": expected.get("health"),
014976:                     "restart_count": baseline_restarts,
014977:                     "restart_policy": expected.get("restart_policy"),
014978:                 },
014979:             )
014980:         except RuntimeError as exc:
014981:             message = str(exc).replace("New image source", "Restored app").replace(
014982:                 "source switch", "restore"
014983:             )
014984:             raise RuntimeError(message) from exc
014985:         verified += 1
014986:     return verified

017422: 
017423: @app.post("/api/app-update")
017424: def app_update(data: AppUpdateRequest, request: Request):
017425:     require_auth(request)
017426: 
017427:     if scan_state.get("state") == "scanning":
017428:         raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Wait until the current update check is finished")
017429: 
017430:     app_item = find_scanned_app(data.stack_key)
017431:     if not app_item:
017432:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="App not found in current scan")
017433: 
017434:     if not app_item.get("can_image_update") and not app_item.get("can_version_update"):
017435:         raise HTTPException(
017436:             status_code=400,
017437:             detail="This update is currently blocked by its update policy",
017438:         )
017439: 
017440:     if has_pending_app_scan():
017441:         raise HTTPException(
017442:             status_code=409,
017443:             detail="Wait until the post-update verification scan is finished",
017444:         )
017445: 
017446:     if not app_update_lock.acquire(blocking=False):
017447:         raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Another app update is already running")
017448: 
017449:     if has_pending_app_scan():
017450:         app_update_lock.release()
017451:         raise HTTPException(
017452:             status_code=409,
017453:             detail="Wait until the post-update verification scan is finished",
017454:         )
017455: 
017456:     requested_backup_mode = str(data.backup_mode or "none").strip().lower()
017457:     if requested_backup_mode not in {"none", "quick", "full"}:
017458:         app_update_lock.release()
017459:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid backup mode")
017460: 
017461:     # If this app is already configured for automatic updating, the user has
017462:     # already chosen the backup rule. A faster manual click must use exactly
017463:     # that saved rule instead of opening/accepting a second backup decision.
017464:     auto_policy = get_monitor_policy(data.stack_key)
017465:     auto_rule_applies = bool(auto_policy.get("auto_enabled")) and str(
017466:         auto_policy.get("mode") or ""
017467:     ) in {"upgrade", "follow"}
017468:     if auto_rule_applies:
017469:         backup_mode = _normalize_backup_mode(auto_policy.get("auto_backup_mode"), "none")
017470:     else:
017471:         backup_mode = _normalize_backup_mode(requested_backup_mode, "none")
017472: 
017473:     update_error = None
017474:     result = None
017475:     backup_result = None
017476:     begin_action_progress(data.stack_key, "update", app_item)
017477: 
017478:     try:
017479:         if backup_mode != "none":
017480:             update_action_progress(data.stack_key, determinate=False, phase="backup")
017481:             backup_result = create_pre_update_backup(
017482:                 app_item,
017483:                 backup_mode,
017484:                 stack_key=data.stack_key,
017485:             )
017486:             update_action_progress(data.stack_key, determinate=False, phase="starting")
017487: 
017488:         if app_item.get("can_version_update"):
017489:             result = perform_version_update(app_item)
017490:         else:
017491:             result = perform_image_update(app_item)
017492:         if isinstance(result, dict) and backup_result:
017493:             result["backup"] = backup_result
017494:         finish_action_progress(data.stack_key, True)
017495:     except RuntimeError as exc:
017496:         update_error = exc
017497:         finish_action_progress(data.stack_key, False, str(exc))
017498:     finally:
017499:         # MANUAL UPDATE RULE:
017500:         # Never start a full/interval scan here. Queue exactly one targeted scan
017501:         # for this stack while the Docker-operation lock is still held. The
017502:         # pending marker then blocks the 1/6/12/24-hour scheduler until this one
017503:         # app verification has completed.
017504:         schedule_app_scan(
017505:             data.stack_key,
017506:             verification_result=result if update_error is None else None,
017507:         )
017508:         app_update_lock.release()
017509: 
017510:     if update_error is not None:
017511:         raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(update_error)) from update_error
017512: 
017513:     return {
017514:         "success": True,
017515:         "app": app_item.get("name"),
017516:         **result,
017517:     }

016928: @app.put("/api/app-policy")
016929: def app_policy(data: AppPolicyRequest, request: Request):
016930:     require_auth(request)
016931:     reject_mutation_during_scan()
016932: 
016933:     app_item = find_scanned_app(data.stack_key)
016934:     if not app_item:
016935:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="App not found in current scan")
016936: 
016937:     mode = str(data.mode or "").strip().lower()
016938:     if mode not in {"fixed", "upgrade", "follow", "notify"}:
016939:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unsupported update policy")
016940: 
016941:     available_tags = policy_available_tags(app_item)
016942:     target_tag = str(data.target_tag or "").strip() or None
016943: 
016944:     if mode in {"upgrade", "follow", "notify"} and not app_item.get("compose_project"):
016945:         raise HTTPException(
016946:             status_code=400,
016947:             detail="ZimaOS Compose app ID is missing for this app",
016948:         )
016949: 
016950:     if mode == "upgrade":
016951:         if not target_tag:
016952:             raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Select a target version")
016953:         if target_tag not in available_tags:
016954:             raise HTTPException(
016955:                 status_code=400,
016956:                 detail="The selected version is not available in the current scan",
016957:             )
016958:     else:
016959:         target_tag = None
016960: 
016961:     auto_time = _normalize_auto_time(data.auto_time)
016962:     if str(data.auto_time or auto_time).strip() != auto_time:
016963:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid automatic update time")
016964: 
016965:     auto_days = _normalize_auto_days(data.auto_days)
016966:     requested_days = data.auto_days if isinstance(data.auto_days, list) else auto_days
016967:     if any((not isinstance(day, int)) or day < 0 or day > 6 for day in requested_days):
016968:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid automatic update weekdays")
016969: 
016970:     auto_timezone = _normalize_auto_timezone(data.auto_timezone)
016971:     requested_timezone = str(data.auto_timezone or auto_timezone).strip()
016972:     if requested_timezone and requested_timezone != auto_timezone:
016973:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid automatic update timezone")
016974: 
016975:     auto_enabled = bool(data.auto_enabled)
016976:     if auto_enabled and mode not in {"upgrade", "follow"}:
016977:         raise HTTPException(
016978:             status_code=400,
016979:             detail="Automatic updates require 'Switch to a new version' or 'Follow tag / automatic'",
016980:         )
016981: 
016982:     auto_backup_mode = _normalize_backup_mode(data.auto_backup_mode, "none")
016983:     requested_backup_mode = str(data.auto_backup_mode or auto_backup_mode).strip().lower()
016984:     if requested_backup_mode not in {"none", "quick", "full"}:
016985:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid automatic backup mode")
016986: 
016987:     saved = save_monitor_policy(
016988:         data.stack_key,
016989:         mode,
016990:         target_tag,
016991:         auto_enabled=auto_enabled,
016992:         auto_immediate=bool(data.auto_immediate),
016993:         auto_time=auto_time,
016994:         auto_days=auto_days,
016995:         auto_timezone=auto_timezone,
016996:         auto_backup_mode=auto_backup_mode,
016997:     )
016998: 
016999:     with scan_lock:
017000:         for current in scan_state.get("apps") or []:
017001:             if current.get("stack_key") == data.stack_key:
017002:                 apply_monitor_policy_fields(current)
017003:                 break
017004:         save_json(SCAN_FILE, scan_state)
017005: 
017006:     return {
017007:         "success": True,
017008:         "stack_key": data.stack_key,
017009:         "policy": saved,
017010:     }
017011: 
017012: 

======================================================================
casaos_compose_project_exists
======================================================================
010990: 
010991: 
010992: def casaos_compose_project_exists(compose_project):
010993:     """
010994:     Return True/False when the ZimaOS Compose list can be read.
010995:     Return None if the App Management list is temporarily unavailable.
010996:     """
010997:     try:
010998:         status, raw = casaos_request(
010999:             "/v2/app_management/compose",
011000:             method="GET",
011001:             accept="application/json",
011002:             timeout=20,
011003:         )
011004:         if status != 200:
011005:             return None
011006:         payload = json.loads(raw or "{}")
011007:         data = payload.get("data") or {}
011008:         if not isinstance(data, dict):
011009:             return None
011010:         return str(compose_project) in data
011011:     except Exception:
011012:         return None
011013: 
011014:
